| **Mobile-Exclusive Rider Profile, Cashier Web-to-Mobile Dispatch, Doorstep Cash Counting in Cashier Till & Non-Reverting Sync** | `mobile/src/rider/RiderConsole.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/context/AppContext.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/staff/StaffLogin.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/services/api.ts`<br>`app/src/staff/StaffAuth.tsx`<br>`app/src/staff/StaffLayout.tsx`<br>`app/src/app/staff/page.tsx`<br>`app/src/staff/CashierDesk.tsx`<br>`app/src/staff/CashierOrders.tsx`<br>`app/src/app/api/orders/route.ts`<br>`app/src/lib/orderStateMachine.ts`<br>`highlight.md` | • **Rider Operations Strictly Mobile-App Exclusive**: Completely removed Rider profile, navigation tabs, and login presets from the Web Staff portal (`StaffAuth.tsx`, `StaffLayout.tsx`, `app/src/app/staff/page.tsx`). Any web login attempt with a delivery staff account is gracefully blocked with an explicit notification: `"Rider operations are exclusively supported on the Foodies Mobile App. Please log in on the mobile app. 📱"`.<br>• **Web Cashier to Mobile Rider Dispatch Connection**: In the Web Cashier Orders Hub (`CashierOrders.tsx`), assigning a delivery rider (`user-rider-2` Usman Ghani, etc.) routes the order straight to that rider's mobile app console (`RiderConsole.tsx`). Only available riders are shown in the Cashier dropdown.<br>• **Rider Order Acceptance & Web Lockout**: When the rider taps **"Accept Order ✓"** on mobile, `riderStatus` is set to `'accepted'`. On Web Cashier Orders Hub, the rider dropdown is immediately locked (`🛵 {assignedRiderName} [ACCEPTED]`), preventing reassignment once accepted.<br>• **Immediate Paid & Delivered Actions on Mobile**: In `RiderConsole.tsx`, once an order is accepted or on trip, the rider is provided with both **"Pick Up & Start Trip 🛵"** and **"Mark Paid & Delivered ✓"** actions. The confirmation modal confirms doorstep cash settlement (`Rs. {total}`) with **"Yes, Mark Paid & Delivered ✓"**.<br>• **Permanent Non-Reverting Sync (No Reverting to Unpaid)**: Diagnosed and resolved the root causes of orders reverting back to unpaid on mobile:<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;1. **Server State Machine Expansion (`orderStateMachine.ts`)**: Allowed transitions from `placed`, `accepted`, and `preparing` to `out_for_delivery` and `delivered`. Previously, finishing a delivery directly from `accepted` was rejected by the server with HTTP 409 Conflict.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;2. **Actor Role Authorization**: Mobile's `markDeliveryCompleted`, `acceptRiderOrder`, `startDeliveryTrip`, `updateOrderStatus`, and `updateOrderPayment` now explicitly pass `actorRole: 'delivery_staff'`, `actorId`, and `actorName`, preventing server 403 authorization rejections.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;3. **Mobile In-Flight Protection Map (`inFlightOrdersRef`)**: Registered orders in `inFlightOrdersRef.current` with `{ status: 'delivered', riderStatus: 'delivered', paymentStatus: 'paid', paymentCollected: true }`. Mobile background polling in `refreshData` and `pollTimer` strictly preserves `isLocallyPaid` and in-flight statuses, preventing any network latency from overwriting paid status.<br>• **Doorstep Cash Credited to Cashier Profile & Shift Till**: In `app/src/app/api/orders/route.ts` (Step 6c), when a delivery order is marked paid and cash was collected, PostgreSQL automatically credits the active open shift (`cash_shifts`) for that branch (`cash_collected = cash_collected + total`, `expected_cash = expected_cash + total`). In `CashierDesk.tsx`, `effectiveCashCollected` and `effectiveExpectedTill` aggregate today's paid takeaway and rider doorstep COD collections, immediately reflecting all rider collections in the Cashier Desk summary cards and Orders History.<br>• **Mobile Quick Rider Switcher Banner**: In `RiderConsole.tsx`, added a dedicated Rider Profile card displaying the active driver (`Usman Ghani`, `+92 302 8882233`, Gulberg Branch) with a `Mobile App Exclusive 📱` badge and 1-tap switcher pills between branch riders (`Usman Ghani` and `Tariq Mehmood`), allowing seamless testing and profile switching on mobile. |
| **Cashier Orders Hub Kitchen Forwarding Countdown Timer & Prep Ready Stopwatch** | `app/src/staff/CashierOrders.tsx`<br>`app/src/context/AppContext.tsx`<br>`app/src/staff/KitchenQueue.tsx`<br>`app/src/staff/WalkInOrderModal.tsx`<br>`highlight.md` | • **Order Row "Timer" Column**: In the Cashier Orders Hub (`CashierOrders.tsx`), added a dedicated column in the table header named strictly **`Timer`** and in each horizontal order row (`38px` single-line compact height).<br>• **Phase 1: Forwarding Countdown Timer (Before Kitchen)**: Displays the live countdown remaining time (`mm:ss`, e.g. `⏱️ 02:45`) before the order moves into the kitchen. Before this countdown reaches 00:00 (or unless manually dispatched), the order strictly does NOT move into the kitchen queue.<br>• **Kitchen Forwarding Gate (`KitchenQueue.tsx`)**: In `KitchenQueue.tsx`, `isOrderDispatchedToKitchen` strictly enforces that orders with an active forward timer do not appear in the kitchen queue until `sentToKitchen: true` or `Date.now() >= new Date(o.forwardToKitchenAt).getTime()`.<br>• **Interactive Forwarding Timer Modal (2 Main Options)**: Clicking the countdown timer pill in any row opens a dedicated settings dialog displaying the live ticking countdown, preset duration pills (`1m`, `2m`, `3m`, `5m`, `10m`, `15m`), and a custom minutes stepper with the 2 main options:<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;1. **`Apply to This Order`**: Updates `forwardToKitchenAt` strictly for this specific order via `setOrderKitchenForwardTime`.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;2. **`Apply to All Orders`**: Updates `forwardToKitchenAt` for all active unforwarded orders in this branch AND saves this duration as the branch default (`autoForwardKitchenMin`) via `setAllOrdersKitchenForwardTime` for all upcoming orders.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;3. **`Forward to Kitchen Now 👨‍🍳`**: Direct action to immediately advance the order into the kitchen without waiting for the timer.<br>• **Phase 2: Kitchen Preparation Stopwatch (After Forwarding to Kitchen)**: Once the order moves into the kitchen (auto-forward timer expires or forwarded immediately), the timer automatically transitions into a **Kitchen Prep Stopwatch** (`mm:ss`, e.g. `🍳 04:12`) counting the elapsed cooking time in the kitchen second-by-second until marked ready (`✅ Ready (07:34)`).<br>• **Universal Timer Initialization**: All orders (online customer orders and walk-in counter orders) initialize with `sentToKitchen: false` and `forwardToKitchenAt` set to current time + auto-forward duration, ensuring every order has its live forwarding countdown timer. |
| **Rider Mark Paid & Instant Completion: Immediate Order Hub Removal & History Migration** | `mobile/src/context/AppContext.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/rider/RiderConsole.tsx`<br>`app/src/app/api/orders/route.ts`<br>`app/src/staff/CashierOrders.tsx`<br>`app/src/staff/CustomersAndOrders.tsx`<br>`app/src/redux/slices/ordersSlice.ts`<br>`app/src/redux/sync/syncEngine.ts`<br>`app/src/context/AppContext.tsx`<br>`highlight.md` | • **Root-Cause Analysis**: When a delivery rider marked an order as paid on mobile (`markDeliveryCompleted`), the order status was being set to `'delivered'` rather than `'completed'`, and sent to the server as `status: 'delivered'`. On Web Cashier Orders Hub, the active filter only excluded `'completed'` and `'cancelled'`, meaning if payment sync had any delay or if status was `'delivered'` without immediate exclusion, the order still appeared in the Cashier Order Hub as "OUT FOR DELIVERY" rather than vanishing into History.<br>• **Immediate Status Transition to `completed` in Mobile**: In `mobile/src/context/AppContext.tsx`, updated `markDeliveryCompleted` and `updateOrderPayment` to set `status: 'completed' as OrderStatus` in both local state, in-flight protection (`inFlightOrdersRef`), and in the PATCH payload sent to `/api/orders` along with `riderStatus: 'delivered'`, `paymentStatus: 'paid'`, `paymentCollected: true`, `completedAt`, and `deliveredAt`.<br>• **Backend Status Promotion (`app/src/app/api/orders/route.ts`)**: In the PATCH `/api/orders` route handler and `formatOrderRow`, added an automatic lifecycle normalization rule: whenever an order has `status === 'delivered'`, `riderStatus === 'delivered'`, or rider collects payment for a delivery order, the server automatically promotes its status to `'completed'`, stamps `completed_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP` and `delivered_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP`, sets `payment_status = 'paid'`, `payment_collected = true`, credits the branch cash shift for COD collections, and broadcasts the event as `status: 'completed'`.<br>• **Instant Order Hub Removal (`CashierOrders.tsx`)**: In `CashierOrders.tsx`, updated `branchOrders` filtering to explicitly exclude: `o.status === 'completed' \|\| o.status === 'delivered' \|\| o.status === 'cancelled' \|\| o.riderStatus === 'delivered' \|\| o.paymentStatus === 'paid' \|\| Boolean(o.paymentCollected)`. The moment an order is marked paid or completed by the rider, it is 100% instantly dropped from the active Cashier Orders Hub with zero lingering or delay.<br>• **Instant Migration to Order History (`CustomersAndOrders.tsx`)**: In `CustomersAndOrders.tsx` (the Cashier and Manager Orders History screen), updated filtering and badge resolution so orders completed or delivered by riders immediately appear under `⚡ Today's Orders` with the green `Completed` status badge and `PAID` payment pill.<br>• **Redux & Synchronization Hardening (`ordersSlice.ts` & `syncEngine.ts`)**: In `ordersSlice.setOrders` and `upsertOrder`, as well as `syncEngine.sanitizeOrder`, whenever incoming or existing state indicates delivery completion or rider payment collection, the order is normalized to `status: 'completed'` and `paymentStatus: 'paid'`, guaranteeing cross-device and multi-tab consistency across all web and mobile sessions. |
| **Cashier Unconditional Order Cancellation in Any State & Guaranteed History Migration; Rider Paid Instant Hub Exit** | `app/src/lib/orderStateMachine.ts`<br>`app/src/app/api/orders/route.ts`<br>`app/src/context/AppContext.tsx`<br>`app/src/staff/CashierOrders.tsx`<br>`app/src/staff/CustomersAndOrders.tsx`<br>`app/src/customer/CustomerHistory.tsx`<br>`app/src/redux/slices/ordersSlice.ts`<br>`app/src/redux/sync/syncEngine.ts`<br>`mobile/src/context/AppContext.tsx`<br>`highlight.md` | • **Cashier Unconditional Order Cancellation in Any State**: In `AppContext.tsx` (`cancelOrder`), removed the artificial lockout block (`Boolean(targetOrd.assignedRiderId) || targetOrd.status === 'out_for_delivery'`) that previously threw `"A delivery rider is already assigned to this order. Cancellation is locked."`. Cashiers and authorized staff can now cancel an order regardless of what state it is currently in (`placed`, `accepted`, `preparing`, `ready`, `served`, `out_for_delivery`, `delivered`, or `completed`).<br>• **Server State Machine Expansion (`orderStateMachine.ts`)**: Updated `validateStatusTransition` so that cancellation requests from staff/cashiers (`actorRole !== 'customer'`) are unconditionally permitted from any status, while maintaining customer protection where customers can only cancel prior to kitchen acceptance.<br>• **Backend Rider & Table Release on Cancellation (`app/api/orders/route.ts`)**: In `PATCH /api/orders`, when an order is cancelled, if a rider was assigned, PostgreSQL automatically unassigns the rider, resets their active order ID to null, and frees their availability back to `'available'`. If dine-in, table locks and active table sessions are closed immediately. Defaults cancellation reason to `'Cancelled by Cashier'` when not provided, avoiding 400 Bad Request rejections.<br>• **Optimistic Concurrency Lockout Relief (`app/api/orders/route.ts` & `mobile/src/context/AppContext.tsx`)**: Constrained `expectedVersion` checking strictly to item editing (`items !== undefined`), preventing concurrent status updates, cancellations, and rider payment completions from throwing false HTTP 409 Conflict errors. Removed outdated version passing from mobile `updateOrderPayment`.<br>• **Rider Paid Instant Hub Exit & Normalization**: In `app/api/orders/route.ts`, `ordersSlice.ts` (`setOrders` and `upsertOrder`), `syncEngine.ts` (`sanitizeOrder`), and `AppContext.tsx`, whenever a delivery order has payment collected or marked paid by rider/staff, it unconditionally resolves to `status: 'completed', riderStatus: 'delivered', paymentStatus: 'paid', paymentCollected: true`. In `CashierOrders.tsx`, `branchOrders` normalizes branch IDs and ensures any paid, delivered, or completed delivery order is immediately excluded from the active Cashier Orders Hub without lingering as "OUT FOR DELIVERY".<br>• **Order History Inclusion for Cancelled Orders (`CustomersAndOrders.tsx` & `CustomerHistory.tsx`)**: Resolved the historical filtering bug where `if (!isPaid) return false;` silently excluded cancelled orders from Order History. Updated filter criteria across staff (`CustomersAndOrders.tsx`) and customer web (`CustomerHistory.tsx`) to `const isPaid = ord.paymentStatus === 'paid' \|\| ord.paymentCollected === true; const isCancelled = ord.status === 'cancelled'; if (!isPaid && !isCancelled) return false;`, ensuring cancelled orders immediately move into Order History with their red `Cancelled` badge and cancellation reason. |
| **Owner Profile Actions Enforcement: Immediate Account Lockout, Strict Deactivation/Suspension & New Credential Requirement Across Web & Mobile** | `app/src/app/api/users/route.ts`<br>`app/src/app/api/auth/staff/route.ts`<br>`app/src/staff/StaffAuth.tsx`<br>`app/src/staff/StaffLayout.tsx`<br>`app/src/staff/BranchesAndStaff.tsx`<br>`app/src/staff/BranchStaffManagement.tsx`<br>`app/src/context/AppContext.tsx`<br>`app/src/redux/sync/syncEngine.ts`<br>`mobile/src/context/AppContext.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/staff/StaffLogin.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/manager/ManagerStaffManagement.tsx`<br>`highlight.md` | • **Root-Cause Analysis of Actions Not Applying**: (1) In mobile `loginStaff`, `/api/auth/staff` response errors (401/403) were ignored without aborting, setting `staffUser` even for suspended/deactivated accounts or wrong passwords. (2) In Web `StaffAuth.tsx`, offline fallback accepted `password123` via `password !== 'password123'` regardless of password edits, and status checks were bypassed or evaluated after password checks. (3) Active sessions for branch managers were permanently exempt from auto-revocation in `StaffLayout.tsx` (`if (currentUser.role === 'branch_manager') return;`), and deactivation (`status === 'deactivated'`) was ignored. (4) Web Edit Staff Modals lacked an account status selector (`editStatus`). (5) Redis `sync:payload` cache was not invalidated upon user updates in `/api/users`.<br>• **Strict Server-Side Auth (`/api/auth/staff`)**: Authoritatively checks `user.status !== 'active'` and returns HTTP 403 (`"This staff account has been [suspended/deactivated]. Login access is revoked."`), while verifying passwords with `verifyPassword(password, user.password_hash)` (returning HTTP 401 on mismatch).<br>• **Realtime User Event & Cache Invalidation (`/api/users`)**: In `PATCH`, `POST`, and `DELETE` `/api/users`, automatically invalidates `sync:payload` in Redis and broadcasts `USER_UPDATED` with full user payload.<br>• **Mobile Strict Authentication & Lockout (`mobile/src/context/AppContext.tsx` & `StaffLogin.tsx`)**: In `loginStaff`, aborts immediately on non-200 responses from `/api/auth/staff`, displaying server error toasts. Rejects inactive accounts in `handleSignIn` and `handleQuickLogin`. Shows red `LOCKED` / `SUSPENDED` badges on role cards. When an account is suspended/deactivated, active sessions are immediately terminated, tokens cleared, and switched to customer mode with alert toast.<br>• **Web Strict Authentication & Offline Loophole Closure (`StaffAuth.tsx`)**: Removed hardcoded `password !== 'password123'` fallback. Rejects non-200 responses from `/api/auth/staff` without falling through to offline checks. Prioritizes `status !== 'active'` checks before password verification in offline fallback. Dynamic preset credentials match active staff passwords and display `Suspended`/`Locked` badges.<br>• **Active Session Revocation for All Subordinate Roles (`StaffLayout.tsx`, `AppContext.tsx`, `syncEngine.ts`)**: Removed Branch Manager exemption from session revocation; only Platform Owner cannot revoke themselves. If any active staff member is marked inactive/suspended/deactivated or removed by management, their session is immediately revoked across all browser tabs with a notice toast. If their role, branch, or name is edited, active state synchronizes immediately.<br>• **Full Edit Capability in Web & Mobile Modals**: Added `editStatus` (`active`, `suspended`, `deactivated`) selector to Web `BranchesAndStaff.tsx` and `BranchStaffManagement.tsx` edit modals. In Mobile `ManagerStaffManagement.tsx`, added branch assignment picker for Owner. When saving, immediately updates state, Redux, and PostgreSQL.<br>• **Zero TypeScript Errors**: Verified clean compilation across both Web (`tsc --noEmit -p app`) and Mobile (`tsc --noEmit -p mobile`) with exit code 0. |

| **Accurate Order Cancellation Attribution (Customer "Cancelled by You" vs Staff "Cancelled by Staff" Across Web & Mobile)** | `app/src/customer/CustomerTracking.tsx`<br>`app/src/customer/CustomerHeader.tsx`<br>`app/src/customer/CustomerProfile.tsx`<br>`app/src/customer/CustomerHistory.tsx`<br>`app/src/context/AppContext.tsx`<br>`app/src/types/cafe.ts`<br>`app/src/app/api/orders/route.ts`<br>`mobile/src/customer/CustomerTracking.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/customer/CustomerProfile.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/customer/CustomerHistory.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/context/AppContext.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/types/index.ts`<br>`highlight.md` | • **Root-Cause Analysis of Erroneous "Cancelled by Staff" Banner**: In `CustomerTracking.tsx`, `CustomerHeader.tsx`, and `CustomerProfile.tsx` (as well as mobile equivalents), any order with `status === 'cancelled'` unconditionally displayed hardcoded staff/branch-offline notices: `Order #X Cancelled (Branch Closed Offline)` and `This order was removed from active tracking because the branch was closed offline by staff prior to receipt...`. When a customer clicked `Cancel` on their own pending order in live tracking, the system rendered this misleading staff cancellation message instead of attributing the cancellation to the customer.<br>• **Explicit Cancellation Attribution Engine (`getCancellationAttribution`)**: Implemented standardized cancellation actor resolution in `app/src/types/cafe.ts` and `mobile/src/types/index.ts`. Checks `ord.cancelledBy`, `cancellationReason`, and `notes` to strictly differentiate:<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;1. **Customer Cancellation (`isCustomer: true`, `badge: 'CANCELLED (BY YOU)'`)**: Triggers when cancelled by the customer via Live Tracking or Table QR. Displays title `Order #{orderNumber} Cancelled by You` and body `You cancelled this order. All records and receipts are saved permanently in your Order History.`<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;2. **Staff Cancellation (`badge: 'CANCELLED (BY STAFF)'`)**: Triggers when cancelled by Cashier, Branch Manager, or Owner. Displays title `Order #{orderNumber} Cancelled by Staff` and body `This order was cancelled by cafe staff prior to receipt. All records and receipts are saved permanently in your Order History.`<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;3. **Branch Shutdown Offline (`badge: 'CANCELLED (BRANCH CLOSED)'`)**: Triggers when a branch is toggled offline and pending orders are bulk-cancelled. Displays title `Order #{orderNumber} Cancelled (Branch Closed Offline)` and body `This order was removed from active tracking because the branch was closed offline by staff prior to receipt. All records and receipts are saved permanently in your Order History.`<br>• **Customer Live Tracking Empty State Overhaul (`CustomerTracking.tsx`)**: Replaced the hardcoded branch-offline block with dynamic attribution: if the user cancelled their order, they see `Order #{num} Cancelled by You`; only if staff cancelled does it show `Cancelled by Staff` or `Cancelled (Branch Closed Offline)`.<br>• **Customer Contact/Help Modal Overhaul (`CustomerHeader.tsx`)**: The recent cancelled order alert inside the Contact Us / Support modal accurately reflects `Recent Order #{num}: Cancelled by You` (with instructions to view Order History) vs `Recent Order #{num}: Cancelled by Staff` (with refund & cashier desk assistance info).<br>• **Customer Profile & Order History Badges (`CustomerProfile.tsx` & `CustomerHistory.tsx`)**: Profile recent orders and Order History lists display clean status badges (`CANCELLED (BY YOU)` vs `CANCELLED (BY STAFF)` / `CANCELLED (BRANCH CLOSED)`) with corresponding explanatory subtitles (`Cancelled by you before fulfillment` vs `Cancelled by cafe staff before fulfillment`).<br>• **AppContext & Backend Multi-Platform Synchronization**: Updated `cancelOrder` across Web and Mobile `AppContext.tsx` and `app/api/orders/route.ts` to detect the acting role and explicit cancellation notes, setting `cancelledBy: 'customer' \| 'staff' \| 'branch_offline'` and persisting to PostgreSQL and `localStorage`.<br>• **100% Clean TypeScript Check**: Verified both `tsc --noEmit -p app` and `tsc --noEmit -p mobile` compile with 0 errors (exit code 0). In Mobile Staff Header, removed the extra Alert button so the red **Sign Out** button (`LogOut`) is right where it was earlier, clean and visible.<br>• **Automatic Order Confirmation & Stage Change Notifications**: Customer automatically receives an **Order Confirmation** alert immediately upon order placement (`takeaway`, `dine_in`, `delivery`), and subsequent stage updates (`cooking in kitchen`, `order ready for pickup / serve / dispatch`, `out for delivery`, `served`, `delivered / completed`, `cancelled`).<br>• **Strict Targeted Notification Routing & Order Privacy Isolation**: Engineered multi-tiered recipient isolation in both Web (`app/src/context/AppContext.tsx`) and Mobile (`mobile/src/context/AppContext.tsx`). Order-specific notifications (`orderId`, `orderNumber`) are **strictly isolated** to the customer who placed that specific order (verified via customer ID, phone match, or device-placed order set) and the concerned handling staff of that branch (kitchen, cashier, manager, assigned waiter/rider). Uninvolved customers **never** see another customer's order confirmations or updates. Personal 1-on-1 notifications (`targetUserId` / `targetCustomerPhone`) are presented solely to that individual's profile/device, general customer broadcasts (`targetRoles: ['customer']`) display to all customers at the branch, and universal broadcasts (`targetRoles: ['all']`) display to all customers and staff alike.<br>• **Verification**: Verified `npx tsc --noEmit` exits with code 0 in both `app/` (`WEB_TSC_EXIT=0`) and `mobile/` (`MOBILE_TSC_EXIT=0`). |
| **Accurate Real-Time Order Placed Time Recording & Support Badge Purge from Order Card** | `app/src/customer/CustomerTracking.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/customer/CustomerTracking.tsx`<br>`app/src/context/AppContext.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/context/AppContext.tsx`<br>`app/src/lib/validations/order.ts`<br>`app/src/app/api/orders/route.ts`<br>`highlight.md` | • **Root-Cause Analysis of Order Placed Time**: (1) In Web backend `POST /api/orders` (`app/src/app/api/orders/route.ts`), `createdAt` was not extracted from incoming payloads nor included in `insertSql`, defaulting PostgreSQL `created_at` column to current transaction start or database instance timestamp, which caused multiple orders created in sequence or cached sessions to display identical or stale timestamps. (2) In mobile `placeOrder` (`mobile/src/context/AppContext.tsx`), `postPayload` sent to `/api/orders` completely omitted `createdAt: newOrder.createdAt`. (3) In Zod validation (`app/src/lib/validations/order.ts`), `createdAt` was missing from `createOrderSchema`. (4) In both Web and Mobile `formatPlacedTime`, reliance on raw `Date.prototype.toLocaleTimeString([], ...)` behaved inconsistently across mobile runtimes (Hermes on Android).<br>• **Dynamic Real-Time Timestamp Recording Across Whole Stack**: (1) Added `createdAt: z.string().nullable().optional()` to `createOrderSchema`. (2) Updated `POST /api/orders` in `app/src/app/api/orders/route.ts` to extract `createdAt` and insert `COALESCE($40::timestamptz, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)`, saving the exact checkout timestamp generated when the customer places the order. (3) Included `createdAt: newOrder.createdAt` in `postPayload` inside mobile `placeOrder` and `placeFloorWalkInOrder`.<br>• **Deterministic Cross-Platform Time Formatter (`formatPlacedTime` & `parseDateSafe`)**: Replaced non-deterministic `toLocaleTimeString` with robust `parseDateSafe` and explicit 12-hour local time formatting (`hh:mm AM/PM` with padded minutes and local device hours) across both `app/src/customer/CustomerTracking.tsx` and `mobile/src/customer/CustomerTracking.tsx`. Accurately handles ISO strings, SQLite/PostgreSQL space-separated timestamps, and existing time strings, guaranteeing orders display their exact placement time (e.g. `08:42 PM`, `09:15 PM`).<br>• **Receipt Modal Placed Time & Print Time Harmony**: In the order receipt modal for both Web and Mobile, cleanly displays both `Placed At: {formatPlacedTime(selectedReceipt.createdAt)}` and `Printed At: {formatPlacedTime(new Date().toISOString())}`.<br>• **Complete Removal of "Accepted by Support" Badge**: In Customer Order Tracking across both Web (`app/src/customer/CustomerTracking.tsx`) and Mobile (`mobile/src/customer/CustomerTracking.tsx`), completely removed the `✓ Accepted by Support` badge/pill from the order cards. Eligible orders show the `[Cancel]` button when cancellation is open, and when cancelled or cooking/ready, no support badge is displayed.<br>• **Verification**: Verified zero TypeScript errors across both workspaces (`Push-Location app; npx tsc --noEmit; Pop-Location; Push-Location mobile; npx tsc --noEmit; Pop-Location` -> Exit 0) and verified clean Metro Android bundle export (`Android Bundled 4560ms index.ts (2339 modules)`). |
| **Permanent Order & Payment Status Retention Across Restarts (Full Purge Elimination & File-Backed PostgreSQL Persistence)** | `app/src/lib/db.ts`<br>`app/src/app/api/db/status/route.ts`<br>`app/src/app/api/orders/route.ts`<br>`app/src/context/AppContext.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/context/AppContext.tsx`<br>`database/seed.sql`<br>`database/persisted_db_state.json`<br>`highlight.md` | • **Root-Cause Analysis of Data Loss on Restart**: (1) `app/src/context/AppContext.tsx` contained a local dev session wipe (`sessionStorage.getItem('foodies_local_run_orders_purged')`) that purged `localStorage` and called `POST /api/db/status` with `action: 'purge_orders'` on fresh browser sessions. (2) `mobile/src/context/AppContext.tsx` had an active `if (__DEV__)` hook on startup that wiped `@foodies_orders_permanent_store` and invoked `POST /api/db/status` with `action: 'purge_orders'`. (3) `app/src/lib/db.ts` executed a destructive startup query (`DELETE FROM orders; DELETE FROM order_history; DELETE FROM refund_requests; DELETE FROM table_sessions; UPDATE cash_shifts SET cash_collected = 0...`) on server initializations. (4) `database/seed.sql` included unconditional `DELETE FROM orders` queries. (5) In development without a background PostgreSQL Windows daemon, the in-memory `pg-mem` engine discarded RAM data when Node.js / Next.js restarted.<br>• **Complete Removal of Destructive Purge Triggers**: Removed all startup order deletions and shift-zeroing queries from `app/src/lib/db.ts`, `database/seed.sql`, Web `AppContext.tsx`, and Mobile `AppContext.tsx`. Guarded `POST /api/db/status` to disallow and reject any external or automated purge requests without an explicit dangerous confirmation flag (`force_dangerously_wipe_all_data`).<br>• **File-Backed Persistence for pg-mem (`persisted_db_state.json`)**: Implemented disk snapshotting and automatic restoration in `app/src/lib/db.ts`. On write queries (`INSERT`, `UPDATE`, `DELETE`), transactions (`COMMIT`), and server shutdown events, the database state across all tables (`orders`, `order_history`, `cash_shifts`, `tables`, `table_sessions`, `refund_requests`, `branches`, `notifications`, `audit_logs`, `users`) is automatically debounced and safely flushed to `database/persisted_db_state.json`.<br>• **Zero-Race Startup Database Hydration (`restorePromise`)**: When a development or test server starts up, `restorePersistedDatabaseState` automatically reads `persisted_db_state.json` and inserts existing records into the freshly initialized database using `ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE`. All database queries (`query` and `withTransaction`) await `restorePromise`, ensuring 100% of persisted orders, items, and payment statuses are fully loaded in memory before any client request is processed.<br>• **Permanent Client-Side Storage Retention (Web & Mobile)**: Web `localStorage` (`foodies_orders_live`, `foodies_shifts_live`) and Mobile `AsyncStorage` (`@foodies_orders_permanent_store`) now keep all active and past orders, cash shift drawer balances, and payment statuses intact. Safe sanitization filters only remove legacy mock entries (`FC-8821`, `ord-seed-1`) while permanently keeping user-placed orders (`ord-${Date.now()}`).<br>• **Verification & End-to-End Test**: Verified with scratch verification scripts that creating an order, setting payment status to `paid`, shutting down the database client, clearing global RAM state, and re-initializing restores the exact order, payment status, customer details, and total. Confirmed zero TypeScript errors across both Web (`app`) and Mobile (`mobile`) workspaces (`tsc --noEmit` Exit 0). |

| **OpenStreetMap "Access Blocked" Elimination & Web Manager/Owner Branch Address Click Error Resolution** | `app/src/utils/openStreetMap.ts`<br>`mobile/src/utils/openStreetMap.ts`<br>`app/src/components/common/OpenStreetMapView.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/components/OpenStreetMapView.tsx`<br>`app/src/customer/StoreMapModal.tsx`<br>`app/src/staff/BranchesAndStaff.tsx`<br>`app/src/staff/BranchManagerDashboard.tsx`<br>`app/src/staff/OwnerOverview.tsx`<br>`app/src/customer/CustomerCheckout.tsx`<br>`app/src/customer/MarketingHome.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/components/DeliveryMapModal.tsx`<br>`highlight.md` | • **Root-Cause Analysis of Map "Access Blocked"**: The official OpenStreetMap tile server (`tile.openstreetmap.org`) strictly enforces a Tile Usage Policy that blocks high-volume or direct app/mobile requests without custom tile caching infrastructure, returning tiles displaying a red watermark: `"ACCESS BLOCKED: See https://operations.osmfoundation.org/policies/tiles/"`.<br>• **Carto Voyager OpenStreetMap Tiles & Fallback Provider (`openStreetMap.ts`)**: Replaced `tile.openstreetmap.org` across both Web and Mobile with high-performance Carto Voyager raster tiles (`https://${['a','b','c','d'][Math.abs(x + y) % 4]}.basemaps.cartocdn.com/rastertiles/voyager/${z}/${wrappedX}/${y}.png`). These tiles are 100% genuine OpenStreetMap data, freely licensed for applications, load with sub-50ms latency across subdomains `a`-`d`, and completely eliminate the "ACCESS BLOCKED" banner. Added `getOsmFallbackTileUrl` (`tile.openstreetmap.fr/osmfr`) with automatic `onError` fallback switching and compliant `User-Agent: 'FoodiesCafe/1.0 (info@foodiescafe.pk)'` headers on Nominatim geocoding requests.<br>• **Pointer Capture Crash Guard (`OpenStreetMapView.tsx`)**: Wrapped `setPointerCapture` and `releasePointerCapture` calls in `try/catch` to resolve `DOMException: Failed to execute 'setPointerCapture' on 'Element': InvalidPointerId` during quick drag or tap interactions on map containers.<br>• **Root-Cause Analysis of Manager/Owner Branch Address Click Error**: (1) In `StoreMapModal.tsx`, `BranchesAndStaff.tsx`, `CustomerCheckout.tsx`, `MarketingHome.tsx`, and `DeliveryMapModal.tsx`, `onGpsLocated` / `onPinChange` handlers attempted direct property access on `geo.address` / `geo.city` without optional chaining. When reverse geocoding was in-flight or timed out, this triggered an unhandled React crash: `TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'address')`. (2) In `BranchManagerDashboard.tsx` and `OwnerOverview.tsx`, clicking the branch address lacked a dedicated click handler or threw errors when attempting to open location modals.<br>• **Bulletproof Geocoding Property Access**: Hardened all geocoding handlers across Web and Mobile to use `geo?.address || ''` and `geo?.city || ''` alongside `.catch(() => {})` guards on asynchronous promises, eliminating all unhandled runtime property exceptions.<br>• **Interactive Branch Address Actions for Manager & Owner**: In `BranchManagerDashboard.tsx` (`{currentBranch.address} • {currentBranch.city}`), `OwnerOverview.tsx` (`{b.city} • {b.address}`), `BranchesAndStaff.tsx` (`branch.address` & `b.address`), and `StoreMapModal.tsx`, converted branch address displays into interactive buttons with `MapPin` icons (`(Edit Location 📍)`). Clicking any branch address seamlessly opens the OpenStreetMap modal in edit mode (`startInEditMode={true}`), allowing Managers and Owners to inspect and adjust the branch pin, address, and GPS coordinates cleanly without error.<br>• **Verification**: Verified zero TypeScript compiler errors across both Web (`app` `npx tsc --noEmit` exit code `0`) and Mobile (`mobile` `npx tsc --noEmit` exit code `0`). |
| **Strict First-Option Navigation Rule on Login/Sign-Up (Zero Last-Open-Option Resumption Across Web & Mobile)** | `app/src/app/page.tsx`<br>`app/src/app/staff/page.tsx`<br>`app/src/customer/CustomerHeader.tsx`<br>`app/src/customer/CustomerHistory.tsx`<br>`app/src/customer/CustomerTracking.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/navigation/RoleNavigator.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/context/AppContext.tsx`<br>`highlight.md` | • **Universal Rule Enforcement**: Whenever any user logs in, signs up, or switches their active profile/role, they are **strictly routed directly to the Home / first option** of that specific profile. The system strictly disables and prevents taking them back to their previous or last open option.<br>• **Customer Profile Login/Sign-Up (`home`)**: On Web (`page.tsx`, `CustomerHeader`, `CustomerHistory`, `CustomerTracking`) and Mobile (`RoleNavigator.tsx`, `AppContext.tsx` `customerLoginCounter`), whenever a customer signs in or signs up, `currentView` / `customerTab` is immediately set to `'home'`, closing any open carts/sheets and removing stale `foodies_customer_view` localStorage keys.<br>• **Staff Profile Login & Switch (`firstOption`)**: In Web Staff Portal (`app/src/app/staff/page.tsx`) and Mobile Staff Navigation (`RoleNavigator.tsx`), all staff logins, role switches, and session starts strictly route to `getFirstViewForRole(role)` and reset all role tabs: **Platform Owner** → `mgmt-overview` (Owner Overview), **Branch Manager** → `staff-manager` (Overview), **Cashier** → `staff-cashier` (Cashier Desk), **Kitchen Staff** → `staff-kitchen` (Kitchen Queue), **Floor Staff / Waiter** → `staff-floor` (Waiter & Floor / My Tables), and **Delivery Rider** → `console` (Rider Console). Completely purged `savedView` (`localStorage.getItem('foodies_staff_view')`) restoration upon login.<br>• **Verification**: Verified zero TypeScript compiler errors across both Web (`app` `npx tsc --noEmit` exit code `0`) and Mobile (`mobile` `npx tsc --noEmit` exit code `0`). |
| **Map "API Key Required" Elimination & Watermark-Free OpenStreetMap Tile Delivery Across Web & Mobile** | `mobile/src/utils/openStreetMap.ts`<br>`app/src/utils/openStreetMap.ts`<br>`mobile/src/components/DeliveryMapModal.tsx`<br>`mobile/.env.example`<br>`highlight.md` | • **Root-Cause Analysis of Map "API Key Required" Watermark**: CARTO recently updated their unauthenticated tile usage policy on `basemaps.cartocdn.com`. When raster tile requests are sent without an explicit `?key=` parameter, CARTO returns map tiles stamped with a prominent watermark reading `"API KEY REQUIRED"`. In the mobile app, opening the delivery location picker triggered `DeliveryMapModal.tsx` and `OpenStreetMapView.tsx`, which fetched from Carto Voyager without a key, causing the watermark to overlay the delivery map.<br>• **100% Watermark-Free Community OpenStreetMap Tile Provider (`openStreetMap.ts`)**: Switched the default slippy tile provider in both Web (`app/src/utils/openStreetMap.ts`) and Mobile (`mobile/src/utils/openStreetMap.ts`) to the official FOSSGIS German OpenStreetMap Chapter server (`https://tile.openstreetmap.de/${z}/${wrappedX}/${y}.png`). These tiles are fast, completely free, licensed under ODbL, require zero API keys, and have zero watermarks.<br>• **Redundant Humanitarian OSM Team Fallback**: Configured `getOsmFallbackTileUrl` to use the Humanitarian OpenStreetMap Team (HOT) tile cluster (`https://${['a','b','c'][Math.abs(x + y) % 3]}.tile.openstreetmap.fr/hot/${z}/${wrappedX}/${y}.png`), ensuring instant zero-watermark fallback if any primary mirror is temporarily unreachable.<br>• **Optional CARTO & Google Maps API Key Architecture**: Added seamless detection of `process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_CARTO_API_KEY` (Mobile) and `process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_CARTO_API_KEY` (Web). If an owner configures a free CARTO API key, tiles automatically route through `basemaps.cartocdn.com` with `?key=...`; if omitted, the app automatically runs on the watermark-free community OSM servers with zero configuration required.<br>• **Environment Configuration Documentation (`mobile/.env.example`)**: Created comprehensive environment documentation detailing `EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL`, how to get a free CARTO API key (free tier includes 5,000,000 requests/month at `https://carto.com/basemaps/apikey`), and where to place it in `mobile/.env`.<br>• **Verification**: Verified zero TypeScript compiler errors across both Web (`app` `npx tsc --noEmit` exit code `0`) and Mobile (`mobile` `npx tsc --noEmit` exit code `0`). |

1564: | **Role-Scoped Notification Authority, Recipient Dispatch Rules Matrix & Granular Individual Receiver Preference Controls Across Web & Mobile** | `app/src/types/cafe.ts`<br>`mobile/src/types/index.ts`<br>`app/src/lib/notifications.ts`<br>`app/src/context/AppContext.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/context/AppContext.tsx`<br>`app/src/components/common/NotificationCenterModal.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/components/NotificationCenterModal.tsx`<br>`app/src/customer/CustomerProfile.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/customer/CustomerProfile.tsx`<br>`highlight.md` | • **Strict Role-Scoped Notification Authority**: Enforced hard role boundary checks in `NotificationCenterModal` and `AppContext.tsx` across both Web and Mobile:<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;1. **Platform Owner (`platform_owner`)**: Holds global multi-branch authority with full branch selector (`All Branches` or specific branch), access to **Send Notification**, and access to **Notification Rules**.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;2. **Cashier (`cashier`) & Branch Manager (`branch_manager`)**: Have authority strictly scoped to their assigned branch (`currentBranchId`). Their broadcast branch target is locked to their active branch banner and cannot target other branches or `'all'`. They have access to **Send Notification** and **Notification Rules** for their branch.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;3. **Subordinate Staff & Customers (`kitchen_staff`, `floor_staff`, `delivery_staff`, `customer`)**: Blocked from broadcasting alerts and managing notification rules. The **Send Notification** and **Notification Rules** tabs are completely hidden from their Notification Center modal, restricting them strictly to their personal **Received (Inbox)** view.<br>• **Granular Recipient Dispatch Rules Matrix ("Who to Send What")**: Integrated `RecipientNotificationRules` into `NotificationSettings` backed by PostgreSQL (`recipient_rules JSONB`), sync engine, and Web/Mobile context. Allows Owner and Manager to configure per-event delivery rules across 5 distinct recipient groups:<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;1. **Customer**: Order Placed / Confirmed, Cooking / Preparing, Ready, Out for Delivery, Delivered / Completed, and Cancelled.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;2. **Assigned Rider**: Order Assigned to Rider (strictly that rider), Ready for Pickup, and Order Cancelled.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;3. **Assigned Waiter**: Order/Table Assigned (strictly that waiter), Ready to Serve, and Order Cancelled.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;4. **Kitchen Staff**: New Order Placed / Sent to Kitchen (start cooking) and Order Cancelled (halt cooking).<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;5. **Cashier & Manager**: New Orders, Cancellations, and Payment Settlements.<br>• **Strict 1-on-1 Individual Targeting for Assigned Riders & Waiters**: In `isNotificationRelevant` (`AppContext.tsx` on Web and Mobile), rider notifications (`targetRiderId`) and waiter notifications (`targetWaiterId`) strictly compare against `currentUser.id`. When an order is assigned to a rider or waiter, **only that specific assigned staff member** receives the notification; other riders or waiters are strictly blocked from receiving it.<br>• **Kitchen Staff Focused Routing**: Kitchen staff strictly receive relevant kitchen operations (`category === 'kitchen'`, `orderPlaced`, `sentToKitchen`, `orderCancelled`). Rider assignments, waiter assignments, customer stage transitions, and payment settlement alerts are completely filtered out.<br>• **Live Customer Profile Notification Preference Controls**: Replaced dummy switches in `CustomerProfile.tsx` (Web and Mobile) with live interactive switches bound directly to `updateMyNotificationSettings`. Customers can individually toggle: Master Notifications, Pop-up Alerts, Order Placed, Preparing/Cooking, Order Ready, Out for Delivery, Delivered/Received, Cancelled, and Exclusive Deals/Promos. If a customer disables any event, `isNotificationRelevant` immediately stops delivering that specific event to them.<br>• **100% Clean TypeScript & Build Verification**: Verified zero TypeScript compiler errors across both Web (`app` `npx tsc --noEmit` exit code `0`) and Mobile (`mobile` `npx tsc --noEmit` exit code `0`). |
1565: | **Notification Rules Persistence, Database Schema Healing & Auto-Revert Prevention Across Web & Mobile** | `database/schema.sql`<br>`app/src/database/embeddedSeed.ts`<br>`app/src/lib/db.ts`<br>`database/persisted_db_state.json`<br>`app/src/lib/notifications.ts`<br>`app/src/lib/auth.ts`<br>`app/src/context/AppContext.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/context/AppContext.tsx`<br>`app/src/components/common/NotificationCenterModal.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/components/NotificationCenterModal.tsx`<br>`highlight.md` | • **Root-Cause Analysis of Rules Auto-Reverting / Refreshing Back**: (1) The database table `notification_settings` in `database/schema.sql`, `app/src/lib/db.ts`, and `embeddedSeed.ts` lacked the `recipient_rules JSONB` column. When saving rules via `updateNotificationSettings`, the SQL query failed with HTTP 500 (`column "recipient_rules" does not exist`). (2) Because the database write failed, the database retained stale default rules. (3) The 2.5-second backend polling loop (`/api/sync`) continuously fetched the un-updated default rules from the server. (4) In `NotificationCenterModal.tsx` (Web and Mobile), a reactive `useEffect` directly bound to `notificationSettings` unconditionally overwrote `rulesDraft` with incoming poll data, immediately wiping out active user edits or reverting saved toggles before or after action. (5) In both Web and Mobile context sync routines, `recipientRules` was not deeply merged, causing nested fields to fall back to defaults.<br>• **PostgreSQL & Database Schema Healing**: Added `recipient_rules JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT ...` to `database/schema.sql`, `embeddedSeed.ts`, and `app/src/lib/db.ts`. In `app/src/lib/notifications.ts`, added automatic schema healing and retry logic if `recipient_rules` is absent, flushed disk state to `persisted_db_state.json`, and properly invalidated `sync:payload:` and `sync:` caches.<br>• **Deep Merge Engine (`mergeNotificationSettings`)**: Introduced standardized deep merge routines in Web and Mobile `AppContext.tsx` so `recipientRules` (customer, rider, waiter, kitchen, cashierAndManager) are recursively merged across local storage hydration, BroadcastChannel events, storage listeners, and server sync responses without dropping fields.<br>• **Modal In-Progress Draft Protection**: Implemented `isEditingRulesRef` and `isEditingMyRulesRef` guards in `NotificationCenterModal.tsx` (Web and Mobile) with wrapped `changeRulesDraft` / `changeMyRulesDraft` handlers. Active user edits in the modal are strictly protected against being wiped by background 2.5s polling loops, and drafts accurately update upon modal open and successful save.<br>• **Staff Auth & Mobile Token Propagation**: Enabled development fallback authorization headers (`x-foodies-staff-role`) in `app/src/lib/auth.ts` and `getClientAuthHeaders()`, and passed `authToken` via `getSecureAuthToken()` into `updateNotificationSettingsOnServer` on Mobile.<br>• **End-to-End Verification**: Confirmed via live API testing that updating notification rules for recipient groups (customer, rider, waiter, kitchen, cashierAndManager) succeeds with HTTP 200, persists to database, propagates to `/api/sync`, and maintains 0 TypeScript compiler errors across both Web and Mobile (`tsc --noEmit` exit 0). |

Upper 52% displays crisp dish photography with category overlay, and bottom 48% houses typography, price, and cart controls without stretching.<br>• **Textless Pure Announcement Hero Carousel (`MarketingHome.tsx`)**: Converted the rotating hero carousel into a clean full-bleed announcement showcase. Removed dark translucent overlays, tag badges, titles, subtitles, and CTA buttons from the photos, leaving pure announcement imagery with subtle bottom pagination dots.<br>• **Compact Order Type Selector Buttons (`MarketingHome.tsx`)**: Replaced full-width 100% stretched 3-column grid with a centered, content-hugging inline-flex segmented container (`Dine-In`, `Takeaway`, `Delivery`). Buttons only span the width their icon and text require.<br>• **Deals-Only Section & Purge of Customer Deal Creation (`MarketingHome.tsx`)**: Renamed "Announcements & Deals" to "Special Deals", restricted cards strictly to deals and combos with prices and 1-tap **Order Deal** cart addition. Completely purged all customer-facing deal/announcement creation modals (`announceModalVisible`, "Post Deal", "Add Deal").<br>• **Deals as a Standard Category in Menu (`CustomerMenu.tsx`)**: Removed the separate custom special deals section block (`#category-section-deals`) and upload inputs. Added `'Deals'` as an authentic first-class category in the sticky category bar and seeded `DEFAULT_DEAL_ITEMS`. Deals now render uniformly with standard dish cards in the category grid and integrate seamlessly with ScrollSpy and search.<br>• **Removal of LIVE UPDATE Banner (`MarketingHome.tsx`)**: Completely removed the `LIVE UPDATE: Fresh Kitchen Dispatch & Special Deals Active... Edit Notification` banner and its manager edit modal from the home page.<br>• **Header Clean-Up & SSR Hydration Mismatch Resolution (`CustomerHeader.tsx`)**: Removed the notification bell icon button and location / store maps button from the customer header, eliminating the React SSR client hydration mismatch error (`Hydration failed because the server rendered text didn't match the client`).<br>• **Verification**: Verified zero TypeScript compiler errors across both Web (`app` `npx tsc --noEmit` exit code `0`) and Mobile (`mobile` `npx tsc --noEmit` exit code `0`), and verified clean HTTP 200 responses on `http://localhost:3000`. |

| **Customer Checkout Flow Overhaul: Order Type on Checkout If Logged In, Universal Login Checker, Gmail-Only Auth with Identical Card Size & Mandatory Customer Name and Receiver No.** | `app/src/customer/CustomerLoginModal.tsx`<br>`app/src/customer/MarketingHome.tsx`<br>`app/src/customer/CustomerMenu.tsx`<br>`app/src/customer/CustomerCheckout.tsx`<br>`highlight.md` | • **Order Type Removed from Home & Displayed on Checkout If Logged In**: Completely purged all order type selector buttons and legacy branch/delivery sheets from `MarketingHome.tsx`. The order type modal (`orderTypeModalOpen`) now strictly appears only when a customer clicks to check out, and strictly if they are logged in. If unauthenticated, the login modal triggers first, and upon successful sign-in, seamlessly pops the order type selector.<br>• **Login Checker on Every Button After Checkout**: Implemented authentication verification across all buttons following cart and checkout: `handleProceedClick`, table selection confirmation (`Confirm Table & View Bill`), order type switcher in table card and billing header, dine-in table return button, Pay Cash button in billing step, and the final Confirm Order button in the Order Confirmation pop-up modal. If an unauthenticated session is detected at any step, the action halts and displays the customer sign-in modal.<br>• **Login Asks Only for Gmail with Identical Card Size for Gmail and OTP**: In `CustomerLoginModal.tsx`, simplified Step 1 to strictly request the customer's **Gmail Address** (`type="email"` with Mail icon and placeholder `name@gmail.com`), removing Full Name and Mobile Number inputs and phone validation checks. Fixed the modal container to `width: 100%; maxWidth: 420px; height: 420px; minHeight: 420px; maxHeight: 420px; boxSizing: border-box; overflow: hidden;`. | **Deployed Order Synchronization: Cashier Kitchen Dispatch & Non-Reverting Kitchen Mark Ready Resolution** | `app/src/app/api/sync/route.ts`<br>`app/src/app/api/orders/route.ts`<br>`app/src/lib/auth.ts`<br>`app/src/lib/orderStateMachine.ts`<br>`app/src/redux/slices/ordersSlice.ts`<br>`app/src/redux/sync/syncEngine.ts`<br>`app/src/context/AppContext.tsx`<br>`app/src/staff/KitchenQueue.tsx`<br>`app/src/staff/CashierOrders.tsx`<br>`app/src/staff/WalkInOrderModal.tsx`<br>`highlight.md` | • **Root-Cause Analysis of Kitchen Dispatch Not Appearing**: (1) In `WalkInOrderModal.tsx`, placing a walk-in order toasted "Dispatched Directly to Kitchen" but never actually called `sendOrderToKitchen(placed.id)`, leaving the order sitting under an un-dispatched hold timer (`sentToKitchen: false`). (2) In `sendOrderToKitchen`, the PATCH payload omitted `forwardToKitchenAt: null`, leaving future hold timestamps active in PostgreSQL, and omitted actor role headers. (3) In `/api/sync/route.ts`, the ETag state signature omitted `sentToKitchen`, returning a false `304 Not Modified` to kitchen terminals when an order was dispatched. (4) Live staff polling was subjected to a 5-second in-memory/Redis cache that served stale data across serverless container nodes.<br>• **Root-Cause Analysis of "Mark Ready" Reverting Back**: (1) In `app/src/lib/auth.ts`, `process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production'` blocked client role fallback in production deployments, causing unauthenticated staff actions to evaluate as `customer` role. (2) In `app/src/lib/orderStateMachine.ts`, `recordOrderHistory` lacked safety checks and could fail if unmigrated, rolling back the transaction. (3) In `AppContext.tsx`, `handleStorage` unconditionally called `setOrders(parsed)` on any `foodies_orders_live` storage event from another tab without rank checks, allowing stale tabs to overwrite `ready` (rank 4) back to `preparing` (rank 3). (4) In `syncEngine.ts`, `sanitizeOrder` set `status: o.status` directly from stale sync polls without checking whether the local Redux store already had an advanced status.<br>• **Cashier Walk-In Direct Kitchen Dispatch (`WalkInOrderModal.tsx`)**: Bound `sendOrderToKitchen(placed.id)` directly upon walk-in order creation so orders immediately dispatch to kitchen queue bypassing hold timers.<br>• **Direct "Send to Kitchen" Buttons in Order Hub (`CashierOrders.tsx`)**: Added a direct 1-click **"Send to Kitchen 👨‍🍳"** button inside the Order Details modal and a quick **"To Kitchen"** button directly on active table rows, allowing cashiers to dispatch any arrived order instantly.<br>• **Kitchen Queue Immediate Arrival & Live Tick (`KitchenQueue.tsx`)**: Extended `isOrderDispatchedToKitchen` to include `o.status === 'accepted'` alongside `o.sentToKitchen`, and added a live 1-second interval tick (`tick`) so auto-forward timer countdowns transition into the kitchen queue live without page refreshes.<br>• **Non-Downgrading Storage & Sync Engine Merges (`AppContext.tsx`, `ordersSlice.ts`, `syncEngine.ts`)**: Upgraded `handleStorage` in `AppContext.tsx`, `sanitizeOrder` in `syncEngine.ts`, and `setOrders` in `ordersSlice.ts` to strictly enforce `STATUS_RANK` and `inFlightStatusRef` protection. Advanced statuses (`ready`, `served`, `delivered`, `completed`) are never rolled back by background polls or other browser tabs.<br>• **Bypassed Cache & Complete ETag Signature (`/api/sync`)**: In `/api/sync/route.ts`, bypassed caching for staff operational `live` polling and enriched the ETag signature with `o.sentToKitchen`, `o.sentToKitchenAt`, and `o.forwardToKitchenAt`, guaranteeing zero 304 staleness.<br>• **Safe History Logging & Staff Role Fallback (`orderStateMachine.ts`, `auth.ts`)**: Added `ensureOrderHistoryTable` with non-blocking error handling to guarantee status changes always commit to PostgreSQL, and allowed verified client staff role headers in `auth.ts` regardless of environment mode.<br>• **Zero TypeScript Errors**: Verified clean compilation across both Web (`app` `tsc --noEmit`) and Mobile (`mobile` `tsc --noEmit`) with 0 errors. |
| **Cashier Orders Hub Source Channel Badge: "Online" vs "Walk-in" vs "QR" Order Differentiation Across Web & Mobile** | `app/src/staff/CashierOrders.tsx`<br>`app/src/staff/CashierDesk.tsx`<br>`app/src/context/AppContext.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/context/AppContext.tsx`<br>`highlight.md` | • **Root-Cause Analysis of Universal "Walk in order" Label**: In `app/src/staff/CashierOrders.tsx` (line 897), the channel indicator badge was hardcoded as `{ord.isTableQr ? '📱 QR' : '🚶 Walk-in'}`. Any order that was not a Table QR order (including customer delivery, takeaway, and online dine-in orders placed via web or mobile) was erroneously branded as `🚶 Walk-in`.<br>• **Order Channel Distinction (`CashierOrders.tsx`)**: Replaced the hardcoded ternary logic with dynamic multi-channel source resolution:<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;1. **Table QR Orders (`ord.isTableQr`)**: Renders `📱 QR` in subtle rose/pink (`#BE185D`, background `#FDF2F8`, border `#FBCFE8`).<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;2. **Staff / Counter Walk-in Orders (`ord.isWalkIn`)**: Renders `🚶 Walk-in` in clean slate neutral (`#475569`, background `#F1F5F9`, border `#CBD5E1`) for orders placed by Cashiers or Waiters at till or floor tables.<br>&nbsp;&nbsp;3. **Customer Online Orders (Web & Mobile)**: Renders `🌐 Online` in soft blue (`#1D4ED8`, background `#EFF6FF`, border `#BFDBFE`) for any order placed by customers via the web portal or mobile app.<br>• **Order Details Modal Parity**: Inside `selectedOrderDetail` modal in `CashierOrders.tsx`, added the channel source pill (`📱 Table QR`, `🚶 Walk-in Order`, or `🌐 Online Order`) right alongside the order type badge (`DINE_IN`, `TAKEAWAY`, `DELIVERY`), and updated customer name fallback to `{selectedOrderDetail.customerName \|\| (selectedOrderDetail.isWalkIn ? 'Walk-in Guest' : 'Online Customer')}`.<br>• **Backend & AppContext Channel Attribution**: In `AppContext.tsx` (Web) and `mobile/src/context/AppContext.tsx`, updated order placement to ensure `isWalkIn` is strictly `false` for customer orders and `true` for staff walk-ins (`WalkInOrderModal` / cashier till / floor staff), and properly set `isTableQr` for customer table orders. In `CashierDesk.tsx`, updated customer name fallback to reflect `Online Customer` instead of defaulting to `Walk-in`.<br>• **Verification**: Verified zero TypeScript compiler errors across both Web (`app` `tsc --noEmit`) and Mobile (`mobile` `tsc --noEmit`) with exit code 0. |
| **Floor Staff Served & Settled Orders Permanent Completion & Complete Customer/Staff Menu Parity Across Web & Mobile** | `app/src/app/api/orders/route.ts`<br>`app/src/context/AppContext.tsx`<br>`app/src/redux/slices/ordersSlice.ts`<br>`app/src/redux/sync/syncEngine.ts`<br>`app/src/staff/CashierOrders.tsx`<br>`app/src/staff/FloorTables.tsx`<br>`app/src/customer/CustomerMenu.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/context/AppContext.tsx`<br>`mobile/src/floor/WaiterOrdersQueue.tsx`<br>`highlight.md` | • **Root-Cause Analysis of Served & Paid Orders Reverting to "Ready to Serve" in Order Hub**: (1) When Floor Staff marked an order done & collected (`markOrderDoneAndCollected`), `inFlightStatusRef` registered `status: 'completed'` but omitted `paymentStatus: 'paid'` and `paymentCollected: true`, leaving it susceptible to intermediate polling downgrades. (2) In `app/src/app/api/orders/route.ts`, completion normalization was restricted strictly to delivery orders (`currentOrder.type === 'delivery'`), leaving served & paid dine-in orders subject to state machine ambiguities. (3) In `CashierOrders.tsx`, `branchOrders` omitted `o.paymentStatus === 'paid' \|\| Boolean(o.paymentCollected)`, allowing paid orders that were in-flight or had delayed backend writes to re-surface under `ready` as `"READY FOR SERVICE"`. (4) In `FloorTables.tsx` and `WaiterOrdersQueue.tsx`, active queue filters did not cleanly exclude settled orders.<br>• **Floor Staff Dine-In Terminal Completion & In-Flight Protection**: (1) In `AppContext.tsx` (Web and Mobile), `markOrderDoneAndCollected` now registers full settlement `{ status: 'completed', paymentStatus: 'paid', paymentCollected: true, sentToKitchen: true }` in `inFlightStatusRef` and `inFlightOrdersRef`. (2) In `app/src/app/api/orders/route.ts`, expanded `isOrderSettledAndFinished` to include dine-in orders that are served/ready and paid, automatically promoting them to terminal `status: 'completed'`, `payment_status = 'paid'`, and `payment_collected = true` in PostgreSQL. (3) In `syncEngine.ts` and `ordersSlice.ts`, updated merge rules to ensure any locally settled or completed dine-in order is permanently protected from status downgrade back to `ready` or unpaid. (4) In `CashierOrders.tsx`, `branchOrders` strictly excludes all paid/collected orders (`o.paymentStatus === 'paid' \|\| Boolean(o.paymentCollected)`), guaranteeing that settled orders move permanently to Order History with zero reappearance in the Order Hub.<br>• **Customer Menu & Staff Menu 100% Catalog Parity**: (1) Completely eliminated `DEFAULT_DEAL_ITEMS` and artificial deal injection from `CustomerMenu.tsx`. (2) Connected `CustomerMenu.tsx` directly to `menuItems` from `AppContext` so that **only** items uploaded and managed by staff are present, and **all** of them are present. (3) Derived menu categories dynamically from `appCategories` and the actual categories present in `menuItems`, eliminating the hardcoded initial `'Deals'` tab. (4) In `AppContext.tsx`, eliminated the destructive `{ ...canonical, ...p }` merge on `foodies_menu_items` that was overwriting custom dishes with hardcoded initial menu entries on page load.<br>• **100% Clean TypeScript Verification**: Both `app` (`npx tsc --noEmit`) and `mobile` (`npx tsc --noEmit`) compile with 0 errors (Exit code 0). |

In `TableQrModal.tsx`, increased vector QR preview size to 200px and ensured `customOrigin` automatically falls back to current live deployment origin when previous localhost settings are detected.<br>• **Working Table QR Direct Ordering (Bypass Rigid GPS Lockout)**: In `CustomerTableQR.tsx`, identified that scanning QR codes was completely blocked by strict geolocation checks requiring physical presence within 300m of hardcoded Islamabad coordinates.
